# Complete native Mega Stone catalogue

Every one of the 99 current native stones was reviewed individually against its actual form table, source reward routes, receipts and world context. These counts bind this book to the snapshot; they are not a production test quota or a reason to block a future deliberate roster change.

**Global activation:** carry the Mega Ring in the Bag, use the exact listed base form and stone, and satisfy the current battle eligibility rules. The production Ring check is bypassed in TESTING builds, so such builds cannot prove Ring gating.

**Timing:** this catalogue records source story approaches and exact reward positions. It does not derive earliest access from trainer caps, broad map prefixes or the old timing script. Pickup-specific reachability remains explicit implementation evidence. A stone can be worth finding before its base is evolved. Direct captures can bypass an evolution chain; the level listed in an optional evolution is not itself a Mega restriction.

**Tests:** acceptance statements describe observable outcomes to validate where behavior changes or evidence is missing. They do not request 99 mirrored unit tests, frozen prose checks, or repeated unchanged suites. Audit existing checks/fixtures/artifacts first; preserve independent meaningful regressions.

<a id="mega-001"></a>
## MEGA-001 — Abomasite
**KEEP.** The frozen Seaspray floor is an appropriate local discovery for the Snover family. Preserve its early discoverability; an evolution requirement is not a reason to remove anticipation or other early Ice options.
Item: `ITEM_ABOMASITE`. KEEP every existing world position unless an explicit referenced world/acquisition proposal changes it.
**Native binding**

- `SPECIES_ABOMASNOW` → `SPECIES_ABOMASNOW_MEGA` via [`sAbomasnowFormChangeTable`](../baseline/source/src/data/pokemon/form_change_tables.h#L901). Base: [src/data/pokemon/species_info/gen_4_families.h:5699](../baseline/source/src/data/pokemon/species_info/gen_4_families.h#L5699); Mega: [src/data/pokemon/species_info/gen_4_families.h:5782](../baseline/source/src/data/pokemon/species_info/gen_4_families.h#L5782).

**Exact reward routes**

**MEGA-001-R01 — physical pickup**
Map: [Seaspray_Cave_B1F](../world/maps/Seaspray_Cave_B1F.md); [map source](../baseline/source/data/maps/Seaspray_Cave_B1F/map.json).
Event `Seaspray_Cave_B1F:object_events:005`: (25,21), elevation3, `OBJ_EVENT_GFX_MEGA_STONE`, `Common_EventScript_FindItem`; quantity1. Fixed-layout walkable neighbors: (25,22,elevation3). This is local geometry, not a proven complete route.
Receipt flags: `FLAG_ITEM_SHOAL_CAVE_ICE_ROOM_ABOMASITE` (0x458).
Normal source itinerary: **Western opening geography; exact subpath may need field access**. Routes104/115/116 span separate pockets. A map-level first visit does not establish exact-tile access.
Path prerequisites/conditions: ordinary opening story access; Cut/Surf/bike gate only where physical approach requires it.
Final behavior: Keep this exact sparkle/position/item/quantity1. Existing finditem transaction removes the object and records its persistent flag only after successful insertion. Full storage leaves it claimable; return/reload never duplicates it.

**Base acquisition and evolution prerequisites**
- Snover: MAP_SEASPRAY_CAVE_B1F / land_mons / 8% / authored levels [10, 12]; MAP_SHOAL_CAVE_LOW_TIDE_ENTRANCE_ROOM / land_mons / 7% / authored levels [32, 32]; 4 other snapshot root(s) in [machine catalogue](../review/mega-rewards.json).
- Snover → Abomasnow: level 40 or later ([source](../baseline/source/src/data/pokemon/species_info/gen_4_families.h#L5616)).

**Final acceptance**
- Walk the exact listed reward approaches under their real story/field states; a map-level first visit or a valid neighboring tile is insufficient.
- Check successful/full-storage/revisit behavior for each distinct entitlement, including shared first/retry branches and alternate-route ordering.
- Confirm exact-base Mega eligibility and reversion for the listed binding(s), using a relevant existing valid check or a focused runtime case when needed. Wrong-form and production no-Ring behavior must remain correct.
Dependencies: MEGA-RING, World/acquisition final map tables; actual licensed methods. [Full form/acquisition authority](../chapters/04-acquisition-evolutions-forms.md).

## MEGA-002 — Absolite
**KEEP.** The Jagged Pass find and Lucy’s Lavaridge reward are two deliberate finite alternatives: one exploration, one authored challenge. Preserve both with separate receipts and no repeat award from either.

Normal source itinerary: **Northern mountain route after Dynamo; subareas vary**. Do not assign one earliest cap to an entire long route or multi-floor cave. Exact pickup approach remains a required traversal case.
Path prerequisites/conditions: FLAG_BADGE03_GET for northern Route111 gate; Meteor Falls/Mt.Chimney progression for Jagged Pass/Lavaridge; Go-Goggles for desert; Strength/Surf/Waterfall/bike only where actual route requires them.
Final behavior: Keep this exact sparkle/position/item/quantity1. Existing finditem transaction removes the object and records its persistent flag only after successful insertion. Full storage leaves it claimable; return/reload never duplicates it.

Normal source itinerary: **Northern mountain route after Dynamo; subareas vary**. Do not assign one earliest cap to an entire long route or multi-floor cave. Exact pickup approach remains a required traversal case.
Path prerequisites/conditions: FLAG_BADGE03_GET for northern Route111 gate; Meteor Falls/Mt.Chimney progression for Jagged Pass/Lavaridge; Go-Goggles for desert; Strength/Surf/Waterfall/bike only where actual route requires them.
Final behavior: Preserve the exact one-time grant and its successful-insertion receipt. The source root(s) below supply access and parent-story predicates. First/retry presentations sharing a receipt are one entitlement; rematches cannot grant it again.

**Base acquisition and evolution prerequisites**
- Absol: MAP_JAGGED_PASS / land_mons / 5% / authored levels [22, 22]; MAP_MT_PYRE_EXTERIOR / land_mons / 5% / authored levels [28, 28]; 2 other snapshot root(s) in [machine catalogue](../review/mega-rewards.json).

## MEGA-006 — Alakazite
**REPAIR.** Keep Granite Cave exploration and the authored final Trick Master gift as known alternatives. Repair final tent/stone debt independently under W-TRICK-REWARDS; the early cave stone remains useful to the Abra family.
Item: `ITEM_ALAKAZITE`. KEEP every existing world position unless an explicit referenced world/acquisition proposal changes it.
**Native binding**

- `SPECIES_ALAKAZAM` → `SPECIES_ALAKAZAM_MEGA` via [`sAlakazamFormChangeTable`](../baseline/source/src/data/pokemon/form_change_tables.h#L133). **Exact reward routes**

**MEGA-020-STARTER — conditional original starter**
Source: [Steven starter table](../baseline/source/src/field_specials.c#L555), original Charmander. Receipt: `VAR_STEVEN_STARTER_STONE_DELIVERY`.

**MEGA-082-STARTER — conditional original starter**
Source: [Steven starter table](../baseline/source/src/field_specials.c#L555), original Treecko. Receipt: `VAR_STEVEN_STARTER_STONE_DELIVERY`.
Requires delivered letter, Knuckle Badge, Ring handoff and actual permanent delivery of the qualifying original starter. Legacy encoded-but-still-owed second choices create no stone entitlement yet.

Never create a second receipt ledger or reissue after disposal. Keep Steven until every populated slot is fulfilled. If a legacy save already hid Steven, INTRO-STONES wakes only his Granite Cave object after the actual second delivery when populated second-family slots remain owed.
